HFT visibility is governed by the authoring policy above.","docket_mode":"quarantine_without_hft","docket_ready":true}},"selected_context_units":[],"support_registry":[{"branch_refs":["root_000582/B005"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:2","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_2491e2612882ebb08204","text":"2. **Mention made audible and transmissible.** Fixed `ر ف ع/B005` is announcing or spreading a report, `ر ف ع/B010` is height of voice, and fixed `ذ ك ر/B004` is mention on the tongue. The opening `س م و/B005` designation in 94:0 and the repeated verbal formulation of 94:5-6 create a designation -> voiced report -> repetition structure. The رفع branches supply broadcast and audibility; the ذكر branch supplies what circulates. **Abductive move:** I infer that divine raising operates through human voicing and retransmission; the packet does not state the medium. **Reading change:** **ذكرك** becomes a report made publicly repeatable, not only an invisible increase in rank.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000516/B003"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:retrospective_surprises_md:1","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_66857ebe757042e202ab","text":"The exact recurrence in 94:5-6 changed `ذ ك ر/B003` and `ذ ك ر/B009` from reputation alone into something retained and repeatedly recoverable. Then 94:7-8 supplied behavior governed by that retained relation, sharpening the inferred sequence raised reminder -> recall -> renewed effort and direction.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000516/B008","root_000582/B002"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-4","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_6cce0ae6c6a00bcab0e0","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"94:4:1\",\"root_000582\",[\"B002\"]],[\"94:4:3\",\"root_000516\",[\"B008\"]]],\"grade\":\"reject\",\"text\":\"Anılmanın yükseltilmesi, borç ve hak çağrışımları içinde, yük kaldırıldıktan sonra kişinin hakkını ve konumunu resmen tanıyan bir belgenin öne çıkarılması olarak düşünülebilir.\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":["root_000516/B003","root_001507/B009"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:retrospective_surprises_md:1","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_7a0289e463464926979d","text":"94:7’s `ن ص ب B009` made the vocal side of raised `ذكر` more vivid than it was on first pass: raised mention can become raised-voice chant or proclamation. 94:5-6 also made 94:4 operate as a memory device; the raised mention is immediately followed by a repeated formula, so `ذ ك ر B003/B009` becomes enacted in the surah’s own structure. 94:8 finally changed the status reading: honor is not self-enclosed reputation but a lifted identity redirected toward the Lord.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000516/B007","root_000582/B002"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-6","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_8293ddebae67c35dbea0","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"94:4:1\",\"root_000582\",[\"B002\"]],[\"94:4:3\",\"root_000516\",[\"B007\"]]],\"grade\":\"weak\",\"text\":\"Son ayetteki yöneliş, yükseltilen kimliği kendi ününde kapanmaktan çıkarıp Rabbine dönük bir konuma yerleştirir.\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":["root_000516/B007","root_000582/B002"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-1","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_84d3c2b500eb3e455071","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"94:4:1\",\"root_000582\",[\"B002\"]],[\"94:4:3\",\"root_000516\",[\"B007\"]]],\"grade\":\"strong\",\"text\":\"Yükün aşağı indirilmesine karşı anılmanın yukarı kaldırılması, bedensel ezilmeyi toplumsal yükselişle tersine çeviren bir karşı harekettir.\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":["root_000516/B003"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:3","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_8d674dfb3402f721f3ab","text":"3. **A reminder lifted into durable salience.** Fixed `ذ ك ر/B003` is retaining or recalling, and `ذ ك ر/B009` is a reminder that restores presence. The immediately following proposition occurs twice in 94:5-6, then 94:7-8 converts it into a completion-effort-direction sequence. Repetition supplies mnemonic persistence, while the fixed branches make **ذكر** retrievable guidance. **Abductive move:** I infer repeated wording -> durable recall -> action; the packet gives recurrence and action but not the cognitive arrow. **Reading change:** what is raised is also a reminder kept available for the next difficult cycle, not solely the addressee's reputation.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000516/B004","root_000582/B005","root_000582/B010"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-2","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_991d39580a3615820b6d","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"94:4:1\",\"root_000582\",[\"B005\",\"B010\"]],[\"94:4:3\",\"root_000516\",[\"B004\"]]],\"grade\":\"strong\",\"text\":\"Yükseltilen anılma, yalnızca itibar değil, dilde dolaşan, yüksek sesle duyurulan ve başkalarına aktarılabilen kamusal bir söz olur.\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":["root_000516/B003","root_000516/B008","root_000582/B002","root_000582/B005","root_000582/B009","root_000849/B005","root_001657/B001"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:1","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_ac7722c623cc3a188a87","text":"1. Raised mention reverses the downward load. Lexical evidence: `ر ف ع B002` high rank/elevated mention, `ذ ك ر B007` honorable mention and renown; earlier `و ض ع B001` setting down and `و ز ر B002` burden. Structural cue: 94:2 says the burden was put down; 94:4 says the mention was raised. Assigned role: vertical reversal transforms the addressed person’s public condition from pressed-down to lifted-up. Abductive move supplied by me: the same divine agency that lowers the load raises the name. Reading change: the ayah is not isolated honor; it is the upward counterpart to unburdening. 2. Mention becomes public transmission, not only reputation. Lexical evidence: `ر ف ع B005` announcing a report, `ر ف ع B010` highness of voice, `ذ ك ر B004` mention on the tongue, and later `ن ص ب B009` raised-voice song/chant. Structural cue: the passage moves from opened chest/source of speech (94:1) to raised mention (94:4) and then to renewed activity (94:7). Assigned role: `ذكر` is verbal circulation; `رفع` gives it height and audibility. Abductive move supplied by me: inner expansion becomes outward voice. Reading change: the ayah reads as broadcast and performative utterance, not only status. 3. The raised mention can function as reminder and retained memory. Lexical evidence: `ذ ك ر B003` bringing to mind, `ذ ك ر B009` reminder/admonition, `ظ ه ر B019` memory without a book. Structural cue: 94:5-6 repeats the hardship/ease statement, making remembrance itself a structural act. Assigned role: the raised `ذكر` keeps the relief pattern present against forgetting. Abductive move supplied by me: repetition is a memory device generated by raised mention. Reading change: the ayah makes public remembrance a means of sustaining ease through hardship. 4. A legal/right-document reading is possible but narrower. Lexical evidence: `ذ ك ر B008` rights document or legal instrument; `ص د ر B005` liability settlement; `ع س ر B003` pressing an insolvent debtor; `ن ص ب B005/B006` assigned share and fixed threshold. Structural cue: 94:2’s burden-removal and 94:5-6’s hardship/ease activate an economy of liability, means, and assigned portions. Assigned role: the raised `ذكر` can be the acknowledged document of right after liability is lifted. Abductive move supplied by me: public mention can formalize restored standing. Reading change: the ayah may read as official recognition, not just praise. 5. The shackle-lifting branch creates a liberation image. Lexical evidence: `ر ف ع B009` cord by which a bound person lifts his shackle; earlier `و ز ر B002` burden and `ن ق ض B005` strained joints/back. Structural cue: the shift from load and creak to elevation suggests a constrained body regaining movement. Assigned role: `رفع` lifts what impedes, while `ذكر` is the person’s re-emergent identity. Abductive move supplied by me: raised mention is the social equivalent of lifting a restraint. Reading change: the ayah becomes emancipation into voice and movement.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000516/B003","root_000516/B009"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-3","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_b2a630cb9b8240e51bee","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"94:4:3\",\"root_000516\",[\"B003\",\"B009\"]]],\"grade\":\"strong\",\"text\":\"Hemen ardından zorluk ve kolaylık ilişkisinin iki kez söylenmesi, yükseltilen anılmayı sonraki güçlük dönemlerinde geri çağrılabilen kalıcı bir hatırlatmaya dönüştürür.\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":["root_000582/B002"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:1","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_ec2ad8a69badda015885","text":"1. **A vertical counterweight: bodily load down, social standing up.** Fixed `ر ف ع/B002` is elevated rank and fixed `ذ ك ر/B007` is honorable mention or renown. In 94:2 the burden is set down and in 94:3 it had pressed the back; the mirrored first-person plural clauses and **عنك/لك** sequence place lowering for relief beside raising for the addressee. The fixed branches assign the upward movement to social standing. **Abductive move:** I infer one compensatory vertical economy linking bodily depression to public elevation. **Reading change:** raised mention is the positive counteraction to unloading, not an isolated grant of fame.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000516/B007","root_000582/B009"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-5","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_f3f7bf36e1032d28c038","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"94:4:1\",\"root_000582\",[\"B009\"]],[\"94:4:3\",\"root_000516\",[\"B007\"]]],\"grade\":\"reject\",\"text\":\"Bağı kaldırmaya yarayan ip çağrışımı, yükseltilen anılmayı kısıtlanmış bir kişinin kimliğini yeniden sese ve harekete kavuşturan toplumsal bir özgürleşme gibi gösterebilir.\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"anchor_evidence":[],"anchor_evidence_coverage":{"boundary":"Exact Arabic verifies surface contact only.
